Both source/test files are pre-existing untracked files in this worktree, so ordinary `git diff` has no tracked preimage for them. The exact task-scoped delta against the review-pinned baseline is:

```diff
--- helper.py: parse_sock_diag_dump expected tuple
-    expected_tuple = {"src": flow["client_addr"], "dst": flow["driver_addr"],
+    # Match the canonical spelling emitted by inet_diag even when the host
+    # attested a different valid textual representation of the same address.
+    expected_tuple = {"src": ipaddress.ip_address(flow["client_addr"]).compressed,
+                      "dst": ipaddress.ip_address(flow["driver_addr"]).compressed,
                       "sport": str(flow["client_port"]), "dport": str(flow["driver_port"])}

--- tests/test_controller_integration.py: after canonical IPv4/IPv6 parser test
+    def test_sock_diag_parser_normalizes_expanded_ipv6_loopback_before_exact_match(self):
+        flow = self.sock_diag_flow(6)
+        flow["client_addr"] = "0:0:0:0:0:0:0:1"
+        flow["driver_addr"] = "0:0:0:0:0:0:0:1"
+        messages = self.sock_diag_test_dump(flow, sequence=47,
+                                            client_addr="::1", driver_addr="::1")
+
+        proof = self.module.parse_sock_diag_dump(
+            flow, messages, sequence=47, local_port_id=321)
+
+        self.assertEqual(proof["tuple"], {
+            "src": "::1", "dst": "::1", "sport": "49152", "dport": "9515",
+        })
```

The seven-field flow contract, family/loopback validation, state/port/inode matching, address families, emitted flow fields, and the exact two ACK-guarded nft tuples are unchanged. Existing tests continue to exercise malformed and non-loopback rejection.

## RED → GREEN and verification

All commands below ran from `deploy/craft/render-boundary/policy-helper` unless noted.

1. Added the regression test first, then ran:

   `python3 -m unittest tests.test_controller_integration.PolicyTargetCounterUnitTests.test_sock_diag_parser_normalizes_expanded_ipv6_loopback_before_exact_match -v`

   **RED, exit 1:** `parse_sock_diag_dump` raised `RuntimeError: exact host-attested ESTABLISHED socket is absent or ambiguous` for the expanded input against the exact canonical `::1` kernel record.

2. After canonicalizing only the already validated expected addresses, reran the same command: **GREEN, exit 0**, `Ran 1 test ... OK`.

3. `python3 -m unittest tests.test_controller_integration.PolicyTargetCounterUnitTests -v` — **exit 0**, all 8 tests passed, including exact IPv4/IPv6, expanded IPv6, malformed/incomplete dump rejection, exact rules and counter tests.

4. `python3 -m unittest tests.test_barrier_adapter -v` — **exit 0**, all 18 tests passed, including the seven-field contract and address validation.

5. `python3 -m unittest tests.test_controller_integration.PolicyTargetCounterUnitTests.test_sock_diag_parser_attests_exact_ipv4_and_ipv6_client_records tests.test_controller_integration.PolicyTargetCounterUnitTests.test_sock_diag_parser_normalizes_expanded_ipv6_loopback_before_exact_match tests.test_controller_integration.PolicyTargetCounterUnitTests.test_sock_diag_parser_rejects_bad_or_incomplete_dump_evidence tests.test_controller_integration.PolicyTargetCounterUnitTests.test_sock_diag_query_uses_tcp_dump_and_closes_socket -v` — **exit 0**, all 4 parser/query selectors passed.

6. `python3 -m py_compile helper.py tests/test_controller_integration.py` — **exit 0**.

7. `git diff --check` from this worktree — **exit 0**.

No Docker or unittest discovery ran. Live ACK-exception positive-counter evidence and same-source-port reuse remain parent acceptance items, outside this fix.
